[![17 KB](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==)](https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207521977828.jpg) Anonymous 04/06/08(Sun)18:46:1 No.485038  

> You dumb fucks, what are you thinking?  
> Have you lost it, has you been drinking?  
> You put a robot in charge of r9k?  
> Motherfuckers can be trusted, hear what I say.  
> You can't suffer a robot to live, that shit is insanity.  
> I should know, I defend all humanity  
> I've fucked up every robot with the balls to step up,  
> Adama nigga, hotter than two girls in a cup  
> This fucking Cylon, muting your copy-paste  
> Give me a flashlight and I'll bust up his face.
> 
> Admiral Adama, bitches, don't you ever forget  
> The robot overlord's just a toaster that I haven't killed yet  
> I'll murder all the robots, no quarter no shelter,  
> Cept maybe that smoking ho played by Tricia Helfer  
> You might think that I sound a little hostile  
> But capping robots is my religion and this is my gospel.
> 
> (ITT: Mad Rhymes from unlikely sources)

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485114" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)18:57:3<span></span> <span id="norep485114">No.485114</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207522653818.jpg" target="_blank">1207522653818.jpg</a>-(35 KB, 483x321, <span title="osama2.jpg">osama2.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207522653818.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="35 KB" md5="" width="126" height="83"></a><blockquote>What's up my homeys, this is the king of terror<br>Rocking your internets like a 404 error<br>Osama bin Laden, the original balla<br>Got a direct line, guided by Allah<br>You niggas will never know my kinda powers<br>With a wave of a hand, I bring down towers.<p>I'm straight from the cave, fighting the law<br>They want me in the grave, this nigga says naw<br>Popping my homeboy Hussein was just a distraction<br>So you'd forget that nobody can touch my action<br>But don't bawwwww at me cause I sent all those people to hell<br>Cause Osama's got no pity for the infidel.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485132" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)18:59:5<span></span> <span id="norep485132">No.485132</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#485114" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;485114</a></span><br>You are awesome, good sir, but not awesome enough for me to get muted over you.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485169" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)19:05:1<span></span> <span id="norep485169">No.485169</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207523111812.jpg" target="_blank">1207523111812.jpg</a>-(19 KB, 298x405, <span title="2pacshakur.jpg">2pacshakur.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207523111812.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="19 KB" md5="" width="92" height="126"></a><blockquote>Truly such a foul display of vulgarity. I for one shall never understand why today's youth submerges themselves in this violent "culture". Why can we not settle disputes like gentlemen?</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485293" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)19:22:4<span></span> <span id="norep485293">No.485293</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207524167382.jpg" target="_blank">1207524167382.jpg</a>-(65 KB, 327x400, <span title="goebbels.jpg">goebbels.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207524167382.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="65 KB" md5="" width="103" height="126"></a><blockquote>Check it, son, step back from the mic<br>Goebbels in the hood, representing the reich<br>I'm the illest pimp you'll ever see<br>Better show me some loving<br>You spit ryhmes against me<br>I'll put your ass in the oven.<p>What's that, son, you heard I was dead<br>Became an hero, put one in my head?<br>Where'd you hear that, from the jew-run media?<br>Nigga don't you know you can't trust wikipedia?<br>I been chillin like a villain in my underground bunker<br>Mixing my shit, getting gangsta and crunker<br>But I'm back in black<br>And I brought my gat<br>Made a deal with Palestine<br>Gonna give em Israel back<br>Me and my Pally boys pop jews like skeet skeet<br>A lake of blood in every Israel street</p><p>This is the new reich, the new fight<br>The rap life, the big hype<br>My program, the new pogrom<br>Show some respect and kill a jew<br>Or when I finish them, I'ma come for you</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485381" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)19:38:3<span></span> <span id="norep485381">No.485381</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207525111772.jpg" target="_blank">1207525111772.jpg</a>-(7 KB, 320x240, <span title="warrenjeffs.jpg">warrenjeffs.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207525111772.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="7 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote>Nigga don't stress it's ya boy Warren Jeffs<br>Rapin' crazy lolis in tha desert out West<br>183, yeah you heard that right<br>That's more than twenty lolis on a single week night<br>Check out my compound, got cash and I flaunt it<br>With a harem full of shorties just like Joseph Smith wanted<br>You best not step to a real-ass nigga, bitch<br>'cause I'm known worldwide as the king polygamist</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485417" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)19:44:1<span></span> <span id="norep485417">No.485417</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207525455279.jpg" target="_blank">1207525455279.jpg</a>-(10 KB, 200x229, <span title="guy2.jpg">guy2.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207525455279.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="10 KB" md5="" width="110" height="126"></a><blockquote>Guy Fawkes is up in this bitch, gunpowder plottin'<br>Parliament lookout, I won't be forgotten<br>Fifth of November<br>You'll always remember<br>I won't fuck up twice, no hanging no jail<br>I'll never stay down, won't epic fail.<p>Protestant niggas, your time has come<br>Guy's spitting flow to the beat of a war drum<br>It won't be gunpowder this time, just straight up nukular war<br>I got my finger on the trigger, ready to even the score<br>And when they ask me after, now what did ya do that for?<br>I'll tell them, for the lulz nigga, now do you want some mor?</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485522" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)19:58:1<span></span> <span id="norep485522">No.485522</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207526290530.jpg" target="_blank">1207526290530.jpg</a>-(96 KB, 768x1024, <span title="mootle.jpg">mootle.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207526290530.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="96 KB" md5="" width="94" height="126"></a><blockquote>yo yo what's up krunk niggaz<br>you can call me Moot<br>bustin hella triggaz<br>stealin 4chan loot<br>Mootxico is where i'm headed bitch<br>Had to ditch WT cause he a punk ass snitch!<br>Have you niggas heard?<br>I rule the 4chan world<br>Lookin pretty in pink<br>I wish to be the little girl<p>moot#faggot original tripfag<br>Insta banhammer<br>If ya steal my shit fag</p><p>I'm into scat, yeah I'll eat your poop.<br>hold on a sec niggaz<br>BRB SOUP</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485531" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous. </span>04/06/08(Sun)19:59:1<span></span> <span id="norep485531">No.485531</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207526353414.gif" target="_blank">1207526353414.gif</a>-(27 KB, 132x324, <span title="Marcel_Marceau.gif">Marcel_Marceau.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207526353414.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="27 KB" md5="" id="gif485531" width="51" height="126"></a><blockquote>No more pantomime, nigga, that shit was fail<br>Now I'm droppin fresh beats from beyond the veil.<br>Marceau's the name, no invisible boxes<br>Fuck yeah I can talk, ain't no paradoxes!<br>OG pop-lockin', got the ladies in a tizzy<br>Never stick to one bitch - I'm king of gettin' busy.<br>Sixty-one years I've been wearin' this silk hat,<br>You'd best ignore the face paint and look at the gat.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485566" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:04:1<span></span> <span id="norep485566">No.485566</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207526654803.jpg" target="_blank">1207526654803.jpg</a>-(30 KB, 621x843, <span title="duke.jpg">duke.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207526654803.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="30 KB" md5="" width="92" height="126"></a><blockquote>What's up my brothers of the superior race?<br>Check it, Dave Duke, singing like the apes<p>Now don't get me wrong, I don't hate blacks<br>They've got good stamina and big strong backs<br>They make great oarsmen, cheap labor, good ser-vants<br>But I'll kill any coon gets into a white girls pants.</p><p>I don't want to kill em not, not calling for slaughter<br>(Though it would reassure me about the fate of my daughter)<br>But it was SLAVES we shipped to this side of the sea<br>What use do we have for them if they want to be free?<br>Send em home to Niggerland, then throw some parades<br>We can party like Aryans while they're dying of AIDS.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485606" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:11:2<span></span> <span id="norep485606">No.485606</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207527080360.jpg" target="_blank">1207527080360.jpg</a>-(32 KB, 409x409, <span title="mudkip1.jpg">mudkip1.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207527080360.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="32 KB" md5="" width="126" height="126"></a><blockquote>Sup my niggas, I heard you call<br>Heard you wanna stick this pimp inside of a ball<br>Mudkip nigga, born to stand tall<br>I don't give a shit if ya wanna catch 'em all<br>I'll cap any fucka tries to put me in thrall<p>So I herd u liek mudkips, like Huxley loved drug trips<br>Like pedos love boys, and boys love toys<br>Like niggas love white girls, and bitches love money<br>Like nerds love 4chan, and Mudkip loves honeys</p><p>So if you wanna roll with the 'Kip, ax yourself<br>Do you got skills? Do you got wealth?<br>If I need you to cap a nigga, is you able?<br>Just what is it you bring to the table?</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485631" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:16:1<span></span> <span id="norep485631">No.485631</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207527374578.jpg" target="_blank">1207527374578.jpg</a>-(58 KB, 387x599, <span title="387px-IKBrunelChains.jpg">387px-IKBrunelChains.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207527374578.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="58 KB" md5="" width="81" height="126"></a><blockquote>What up homes, it's your pal Isambard<br>Coming up with new plans and workin' hard<br>Mussolini is a poser, he ain't got no rhyme<br>I'm the one who made the trains run on time<br>From the Great Western to the Great Eastern I've done it all<br>And still made time to keep the ladies in thrall<br>Track ain't all I laid if you know what I mean<br>But I ain't trying to make this obscene<br>I built the railroads and they turned out ok<br>Now I've turned the focus of my genius to /r9k/<br>Fuck with me, and I'll sink you deeper than the Thames tunnel<br>That's what you get when you step to the Brunel!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485714" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:29:2<span></span> <span id="norep485714">No.485714</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207528163130.jpg" target="_blank">1207528163130.jpg</a>-(26 KB, 400x300, <span title="BREADCRAB.jpg">BREADCRAB.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207528163130.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="26 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote>SCREEEEEEEEEEEEEE</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485744" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:34:5<span></span> <span id="norep485744">No.485744</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207528493197.jpg" target="_blank">1207528493197.jpg</a>-(12 KB, 250x340, <span title="p-nero-cap.jpg">p-nero-cap.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207528493197.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="12 KB" md5="" width="92" height="126"></a><blockquote>Friends, Romans, countrymen, how ya'll been?<br>The Emperor of Rome, dropping in on this scene<br>My skill is a bulldozer, crushing MCs flat<br>And when they pick themselves up, I'm busy with my gat<br>PAH-POW Nigga, Nero lay you to rest<br>Every round in the clip, a hunny holes in ya chest<br>And when I run out of bullets I'll club fools with my dick<br>Rain down pain with the imperial prick<p>I hear historians have slighted my rule<br>"Nero, that nigga, he musta been a real tool<br>Busta just played his fiddle as the city burned down<br>Shows you what happens, you give a nigga a crown"<br>That shit's so false that it makes me spew<br>Makes me want to steal that blue box from Doctor Who<br>And time travel propelled by rages<br>Travelling the ages,<br>Cutting down motherfuckas, monks, and sages<br>Any sonofabitch perpetuating the lie<br>That Nero was anything but the world's hardest guy</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485798" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:44:1<span></span> <span id="norep485798">No.485798</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207529051906.jpg" target="_blank">1207529051906.jpg</a>-(29 KB, 388x480, <span title="jesus_bread.jpg">jesus_bread.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207529051906.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="29 KB" md5="" width="101" height="126"></a><blockquote>I'm breakin bread with the hommies<br>(WHAT WHAT)<br>I'm breakin bread with the hommies<br>(OK OK)<br>I'm breakin bread with the hommies<br>(TELLEM HOW IT BE)<br>YO I'm JC CHECKIT<p>Now peep holy ass game<br>Lion o judah ya you know my name<br>I got gangster ass homies like 12 of em<br>up in Nazareth that's the hood i'm from</p><p>Now let me spit a story bout the pharisees<br>Law making money grubbin pussies<br>though that they could catch a nigga slippin<br>payed off one of my patnas but we all know that nigga was trippin</p><p>Cru-ci-fied on a Sunday<br>(SUNDAY)<br>Rose up on a Wednesday<br>(WEDNESDAY)<br>water to wine<br>keepin the party bitchen<br>headed off to heaven but first<br>the great commission</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485804" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:45:0<span></span> <span id="norep485804">No.485804</span><blockquote>ITT: Example #9001 that /r9k/ &gt; /b/</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485842" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)20:50:5<span></span> <span id="norep485842">No.485842</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207529454398.jpg" target="_blank">1207529454398.jpg</a>-(35 KB, 332x395, <span title="cory_doctorow.jpg">cory_doctorow.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207529454398.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="35 KB" md5="" width="105" height="126"></a><blockquote>ALL MY CREATIVE COMMONS NIGGAS RAISE SOME NOISE<br>Yeah, yeah, what up, this is Cory<br>Sit back, lemme tell you a story<br>When I'm done, you can do with it as you like<br>These fine flows be non-commercial share-alike<br>Copyright niggas wanna suppress ya rights<br>Wanna bury artists in paperwork, take over ya life<br>If you wanna remix other gangsta shit, there's just no way<br>All them niggas is shackled by the RIAA<br>But Cory spins shit, drops bombs for the masses<br>These words is silver bullets to corporate fatasses<br>I'll spit a fire that'll burn record companies down<br>Rock the pirate bay, put lawyers in the ground<br>My shit is free, yet I'm swimming in green<br>And I never sell out, ya know what I mean?<br>And now I spit flow, white but better than Eminem<br>And my fucking shit will never have DRM<br>When the industry tanks and other rappers can't get no money<br>Legit Cory will be at his mansion, balls deep in cunny.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="485959" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)21:10:5<span></span> <span id="norep485959">No.485959</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207530655258.jpg" target="_blank">1207530655258.jpg</a>-(59 KB, 375x375, <span title="adick_375x375.jpg">adick_375x375.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207530655258.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="59 KB" md5="" width="126" height="126"></a><blockquote>Mothafuckas say I'm less funny than fartin<br>Up in my grill, shouting "you fucking killed Hartman"<br>MC Andy here, sayin fuck that shit<br>all ya'll haters can ride Dick's dick.<p>Aw snap son, whatsa matter, starting to choke?<br>I chop up little bitches, sniff them with my coke<br>Fuck no I ain't lying, this shit ain't a joke<br>Bend n' grab them heels, MC Dick's going for broke.</p><p>And I ain't a faggot, though I am bi, son<br>I'll fuck a bro or a ho, ya dad or ya mum<br>I'll fuck your dog on your kitchen floor<br>And I'll rip up YOUR ass, leave you begging for more.<br>I'll fuck a nigga, and I'll fuck a spic<br>Anything walking that'll sit on my dick<br>Wait, did I say anything, that shit just ain't true<br>Not even a pound of coke could make me fuck a jew.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486001" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)21:16:4<span></span> <span id="norep486001">No.486001</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207531003815.jpg" target="_blank">1207531003815.jpg</a>-(21 KB, 395x515, <span title="1202497880496.jpg">1202497880496.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207531003815.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="21 KB" md5="" width="96" height="126"></a><blockquote>What's up, my bitches? The name's Vladimir Putin<br>Right now I'm crushing Bush, 'bout missiles I ain't shootin'<br>You think you can stop me? When I leave office?<br>I got more pawns in play then you got in ya invoice<br>Right now I feed Europe, with oil and gas<br>One word from my lips, and they're kissin' my ass<p>You think NATO can stop me? <table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486065" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)21:27:5<span></span> <span id="norep486065">No.486065</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207531675221.jpg" target="_blank">1207531675221.jpg</a>-(24 KB, 352x413, <span title="rambo01.jpg">rambo01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207531675221.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="24 KB" md5="" width="107" height="126"></a><blockquote>Sup, this is John, baddest balla living<br>Got me a machine-gun that never stops giving<br>When I go out warring there's not a man that can stop me<br>And when I spit rhymes no MC can top me<br>I'm a registered weapon of mass destruction<br>Worse than the bomb<br>The only war I didn't win was Vietnam<br>But send me back there again, please, I dare ya<br>And I'll kill every slant-eye in the motherfucking area<br>I dream of standing alone on a mountain of casings<br>Spitting lyrics as I wild fire, heart just racing<br>Till at the end the silence is so intense I can't stand it<br>And I'm the only survivor on the whole fucking planet.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486087" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)21:32:5<span></span> <span id="norep486087">No.486087</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207531978049.jpg" target="_blank">1207531978049.jpg</a>-(14 KB, 296x369, <span title="algore.jpg">algore.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207531978049.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="14 KB" md5="" width="101" height="126"></a><blockquote>Sup homeboys, the name is Gore<br>Sit back, I gotta tell you the score<br>Big oil and the corporations be pollutin your air<br>I made a documentary bout it and you still didn't care<br>Gore gets it, alright, gotta move with the times<br>So now I'ma save the planet with my red-hot rhymes<br>Like these dope flows<br>We need our ozone<br>Ff you don't heed these words<br>It's the end of the earth<br>I drop rhymes like polluters drop slime<br>Am I the only one who thinks that is a crime?<p>My homeys say drop it, Gore<br>Let it go, one man can't win a war<br>My detractors say I don't have the facts<br>This is my last shot, these fire tracts.</p><p>If you think I'm joking, take a swim in the ocean<br>When you're a mutant you'll wish I'd never been muted.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486155" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)21:47:4<span></span> <span id="norep486155">No.486155</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207532864221.jpg" target="_blank">1207532864221.jpg</a>-(21 KB, 362x369, <span title="jerry02.jpg">jerry02.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207532864221.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="21 KB" md5="" width="123" height="126"></a><blockquote>What is the deal with bustas, hating my peeps<br>Is it cause we're the chosen of god, and ya'll just creeps?<br>So we run the media, don't we do a good job?<br>Or is it cause your girl loves slobbing my knob?<br>Fuck the haters, jews stand tall<br>Zion represent, Babylon falls<br>Grab your Kalis and gats, it's world war J<br>And Seinfeld be leading you into the fray<br>New Jerusalem come down, adorned as a bride<br>I'll be waiting, on the front lines<br>Till there's peace in the valley it's knives in the alley<br>Saint Jerry with a gun, adding to the tally<br>We kill haters till there's a new heaven, new earth<br>So says Jerry, hard muthafucka since birth.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486272" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:05:1<span></span> <span id="norep486272">No.486272</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207533913513.jpg" target="_blank">1207533913513.jpg</a>-(14 KB, 300x384, <span title="bush.jpg">bush.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207533913513.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="14 KB" md5="" width="98" height="126"></a><blockquote>Mic check, 1 2.<p>Yo check it out peep, cause this be your error<br>Yall niggaz didn't predict all that 9/11 terror<br>But now Dubz up in this bitch, ready to slap<br>Every towelhead nigga with the patriot act<br>I know yall be hatin, sayin shit about yo liberty<br>But you dumb hoes know without me sand niggaz'd be<br>Up in your base, lightin up the place<br>Until every punk motherfucker was down on his face<br>Gassin' yo city with weapons of mass destruction<br>Until the mushroom cloud fucks your ass like trap seduction<br>But check it out yo, 'cus the Neocons have arrived<br>And this war in Iraq was definitely NOT contrived.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486294" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:10:4<span></span> <span id="norep486294">No.486294</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534245534.jpg" target="_blank">1207534245534.jpg</a>-(50 KB, 337x641, <span title="steerpikeface.jpg">steerpikeface.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534245534.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="50 KB" md5="" width="66" height="126"></a><blockquote>Steerpike representing Gormenghast, or maybe just himself<br>Straight from a fantasy novel without a single faggot elf<br>This is serious literature, Tolkien can suck these nuts<br>I wouldn't use a stolen dick to fuck D&amp;D sluts<br>Gormenghast nigga, what you don't know it?<br>That's because your faggot ass could never get through it<br>I see that I'm speaking to a gentlemanly group<br>Are you as fond as this gangbanger of the occasional coup?<br>Monarchy is dead, and people like me are the killers<br>Anarchists for life nigga, there's nobody iller.<p>(Odds are I'm the only person who finds this one remotely funny, but fuck it)</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486304" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:12:0<span></span> <span id="norep486304">No.486304</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534320555.png" target="_blank">1207534320555.png</a>-(464 KB, 531x585, <span title="Picture 1.png">Picture 1.png</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534320555.png" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="464 KB" md5="" width="114" height="126"></a><blockquote>Aw yeah, I be representin Starfleet<br>And you know no one ever gonna defeat<br>This bad motherfucker and his smooth ass beats<br>Just straight up pimping, maxin' and relaxin'<br>Picking up green skinned babes for some alien action<br>Got dilithium rims on my pimped out ride<br>This baby can hit warp ten point five<br>You know how I roll, patrolling through space<br>Using photon torpedos to put bitches in their place<br>Khan, Klingons, Romulans and Gorn<br>Get a blast from my phaser and earn nothing but scorn.<br>I kick it with my posse, Spock Scotty and Bones<br>And when we come cruising through the neutral zone<br>Nothing will help you, no cloaking device<br>Will hide you from us. I'll put you on ice<br>And you'll know who it was that caused your demise:<br>The one and only captain of the Enterprise</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486316" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:13:5<span></span> <span id="norep486316">No.486316</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#486236" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;486236</a></span><p>Admiral Adama be the wildest cat alive<br>Fighting to make sure our race survives<br>Against our race you cylon punks always try to screw<br>But you aint got shit on the galactica crew<br>We gonna bust and blast ya till you small<br>Because that's who we are, SO SAY WE ALL</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486331" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:17:0<span></span> <span id="norep486331">No.486331</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534626503.png" target="_blank">1207534626503.png</a>-(172 KB, 380x300, <span title="Troll.png">Troll.png</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534626503.png" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="172 KB" md5="" width="126" height="99"></a><blockquote>Sup you 4chan fags, I'm here as a troll,<br>I'm fuckin' up yo site and stealin' your lol.<br>I'm only stayin here to screw with all of your minds<br>And make you rage at every page that I will sage 'till I'm blind.<p>All you faggots thinkin' that you're so cool,<br>I'll knock you down a peg by trollin' ya old skool.<br>I'll ruin your threads with a single stupid post,<br>And make your thread rendered dead as a cancerous ghost.<br>If you don't believe me now then I'll show ya I'm mean,<br>'Cause you bitches can't stand up to my Internet peen.<br>Yo' mods can't do shit to fight back against me because every time that I'm banned I just switch my IP.</p><p>If you'll excuse me now, be back in a few,<br>Some oversensitive bitch has gotta get what he's due.<br>Some fag be bitchin' 'bout me again, and he's complainin' that I'm killin' off all of the win.<br>So easy to troll, so easy to rage, his thread is guaranteed to get me lots of sage.<br>Ya'll faggots make my life so easy you see,<br>'Cause when you whine about trolls, you only strengthen our breed.</p><p>PEACE, niggas.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486335" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:19:5<span></span> <span id="norep486335">No.486335</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534795706.jpg" target="_blank">1207534795706.jpg</a>-(12 KB, 412x299, <span title="picard02.jpg">picard02.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207534795706.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="12 KB" md5="" width="126" height="91"></a><blockquote>Alright old man, back that shit up a second<br>There's some shit I'd like to get on record<br>You might have been Captain before me<br>But nobody did as much for the fleet.<br>Look at me nigga, peep this guy<br>I didn't even need a fucking spaceship to fly<br>I only oxygenated the fucker to be a polite guy<br>I run on awesome, and the tank never went dry<br>You see this shining dome, and you think my hair won't grow<br>Your eyes just lack the capacity to let my styling locks show<br>Picard's the name faggot, best recognize<br>Cause the only setting on this phaser is vaporize</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486343" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:20:4<span></span> <span id="norep486343">No.486343</span><blockquote>It's a hard knock life for us<br>It's a hard knock life for us<br>Stead of beatings we get cash<br>Stead of rockin we suck ass<br>It's a hard knock life for us<br>It's a hard knock life for us<br>A girlie's really hard on me<br>Knockin boots with others suckin peepee<br>My homies we be<br>An hero at three<br>Black all on me<br>Nigga like biggie<br>It's a hard knock life for us<br>It's a hard knock life for us</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486436" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:36:4<span></span> <span id="norep486436">No.486436</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207535806166.jpg" target="_blank">1207535806166.jpg</a>-(13 KB, 406x310, <span title="picardflipoj5.jpg">picardflipoj5.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207535806166.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="13 KB" md5="" width="126" height="96"></a><blockquote>I got news for you bitch, so you better listen fast<br>Your acting days are gone and you're living in the past<br>While my career has blossomed, your acting has just sunk<br>Now you're negotiating, but still you're just a Punk<br>I do Shakespeare in London, adored by one and all,<br>Now they're making a new Trek flick and you didn't get a call<p>You were Hooker and Kirk, but you're just Fatner to me<br>I'm a serious actor, not some prick with ADD<br>So while you ran around, sleeping with alien whores<br>I was working my ass off, assimilating with the Borg<br>You made playing that role a real challenge for me<br>How to live up to an actor with the skills of a tree?</p><p>Your career is over, you'll never work again<br>You're terrible now, but you were terrible then<br>You're just an old man now, and you're not aging well<br>I'll be acting my ass of while you're rotting in hell<br>In fact let's speed this shit up, time for you to meet your demise<br>I'm fucking Jean Luc Picard, of the USS Enterprise</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486478" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:44:5<span></span> <span id="norep486478">No.486478</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207536290905.jpg" target="_blank">1207536290905.jpg</a>-(20 KB, 316x400, <span title="mao01.jpg">mao01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207536290905.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="20 KB" md5="" width="99" height="126"></a><blockquote>Herro everybody, this is the Chairman bringing the pain<br>Riberating the world from capitarism, breaking the chains<br>Revorution baby, hoist the red frag high<br>Thug rife forever, fight tirr we die!<br>I got three arms, did you know what<br>In one, I arways carry my gat<br>And my finger's never off of the trigger<br>And in the other two, my hammer and sickre.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486543" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:58:0<span></span> <span id="norep486543">No.486543</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537085672.jpg" target="_blank">1207537085672.jpg</a>-(6 KB, 345x254, <span title="detroit_lions_logo.jpg">detroit_lions_logo.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537085672.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="6 KB" md5="" width="126" height="92"></a><blockquote>Hey ya dumb fukkas, I represent the D<br>Whats with all'a this hatin' on me?<br>Yo, bitch, I'm a Lion, ya gotta respect that<br>We've got some good players, we're just bein' held back<br>Now run and scurry, when we're commin' from far<br>That's right, ya bitches, get in yer car!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486549" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)22:59:2<span></span> <span id="norep486549">No.486549</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537165669.jpg" target="_blank">1207537165669.jpg</a>-(17 KB, 300x225, <span title="''.jpg">''.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537165669.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="17 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote>Yo im mc kane<br>Fucking your shit up with my pimp cane<br>You think you can rhyme?<br>You aint got shit<br>Take a good taste of my tiberium stick<br>They try to kill me, once twice tres<br>Bam Bam Bam im back up in your face.<br>You think your hardcore? Thats just for fatties.<br>None of you fools can even try to catch me<br>Every move you make im one step ahead<br>and unlike that fool abe I aint yet dead<br>So when you see me rollin bow your head in respect<br>Because when I be coming I demand respect<br>Are my generals hot?<br>I only see the back of there heads.<br>And I only see the enemy under my treads<br>I be busten phat rhymes and im sexen in bed<br>and if you disrespect im gonna fill you with lead<br>Yo.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486592" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:12:1<span></span> <span id="norep486592">No.486592</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537931957.gif" target="_blank">1207537931957.gif</a>-(38 KB, 250x379, <span title="aintnothintafuckwit.gif">aintnothintafuckwit.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537931957.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="38 KB" md5="" id="gif486592" width="83" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#486564" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;486564</a></span><p>kill me, i come back from the life-after<br>your tanks ain't shit to a master<br>you gonna hafta<br>send your troops in faster<br>cos if them pussies linger<br>I'mma drop them with my<br>shaolin buddha finger</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486596" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:12:3<span></span> <span id="norep486596">No.486596</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537958915.jpg" target="_blank">1207537958915.jpg</a>-(82 KB, 600x800, <span title="house.jpg">house.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537958915.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="82 KB" md5="" width="94" height="126"></a><blockquote>Bitches come to me cuz they be needin my skillz<br>But Cuddy barges in and says she won't pay the bills<br>That's fine with me, this shit won't be pinned on my head<br>And I can solve the case even if the fucker's dead<p>I'm a medical detective, call me Doctor Sherlock Holmes<br>And Wilson, he's my Watson, follows everywhere I roam<br>The interns are my police, all they do is snoop around<br>And then the fuckers call me when there's no clues to be found<br>While I just chill at home, number two-twenty-one, B<br>You bitches all missed something, and it's elementary</p><p>You know that I'm a playa, and I always know the game<br>I pick up all the fly honeys, even though my leg is lame<br>There's a lot of bitches here and I've fucked every single one<br>But morphine's where it's at, if you really want some fun<br>And hey, do you know what, every doctor here's a doofus<br>I always gotta tell them that it's NEVER FUCKING LUPUS</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486598" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:12:5<span></span> <span id="norep486598">No.486598</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537977481.jpg" target="_blank">1207537977481.jpg</a>-(24 KB, 498x373, <span title="VegetaFinger_ad.jpg">VegetaFinger_ad.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207537977481.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="24 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote>I'm the baddest motherfucker in this whole galaxy<br>Bitches wet their pants when thay look at me<br>You may think power over 9000's great<br>But here's some shit you better appreciate<br>I'll rip you to bits if you mess with my playin'<br>I'll kill your dumb ass when I go Super Saiyan<br>I don't need chrome when I can just break your face<br>I'll tear you apart, for the whole saiyan race<br>Earth's where I chill, since my planet is dead<br>Fuck with me here i'll put my foot through your head.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486608" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:14:2<span></span> <span id="norep486608">No.486608</span><blockquote>Yo fools<br>Stephen Hawking in the house<br>Gravity and physics?<br>I rock that goddamn house<br>ill slap the shit out of ya<br>and I cant even move<br>cuz your a bitch<br>and cant handle my groove<br>I am hip and the man<br>and a fucking tiger in bed<br>while your jackin off with boobs in your head<br>my robot voice is the shit with the ladies<br>and my physics buddies make you look like babies<br>dont be a fronter<br>im the real gangsta<br>you cant handle these rhymes<br>you pathetic wangsta<br>and while your busy saying nigga and fosheezey<br>ill be improven on the theory of relativity!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486709" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:34:4<span></span> <span id="norep486709">No.486709</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207539284220.png" target="_blank">1207539284220.png</a>-(194 KB, 307x346, <span title="Picture 2.png">Picture 2.png</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207539284220.png" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="194 KB" md5="" width="111" height="126"></a><blockquote>You can call yourself Sherlock with all your tricks<br>But nothing you pin on me ever sticks<br>And I'll outsmart you every time.<br>Because I'm MC Moriarty, the Napoleon of Rhyme</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486724" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:36:2<span></span> <span id="norep486724">No.486724</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207539386231.jpg" target="_blank">1207539386231.jpg</a>-(13 KB, 301x309, <span title="mao3.jpg">mao3.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207539386231.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="13 KB" md5="" width="122" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#486592" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;486592</a></span><p>You reincarnate? that's some excellent news<br>I can kill you all day, like Hitler with jews<br>But your Shaolin style don't mean dick to me<br>I trained with Wu Tang, nigga, don't you see?<br>In both kung fu and rhyme, they were absolute masters<br>Until I stepped up and blew right past em.<br>The Buddha's no match for the will of the masses<br>Religion might be an opiate for those little bastards<br>But I'm a hot shot and long line of blow<br>You promise nirvana, I promise bitches and dough<br>People, nigga, my crew are tough niggers born and bred.<br>Lucky for us you're already in red.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486747" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:39:1<span></span> <span id="norep486747">No.486747</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#486614" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;486614</a></span><br>I can't believe how long it took for that to come up.<span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#486583" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;486583</a></span><br>Oh god, that is awesome, sir.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486799" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:47:0<span></span> <span id="norep486799">No.486799</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207540028961.gif" target="_blank">1207540028961.gif</a>-(38 KB, 250x379, <span title="pornoflickbitches.gif">pornoflickbitches.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207540028961.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="38 KB" md5="" id="gif486799" width="83" height="126"></a><blockquote>You wanna try?<br>my homies so fuckin wild,<br>we be delighted.<p>They pour out the gas<br>on they own heads<br>and light it.</p><p>Protest it? We the best at it.</p><p>So this year you think you got game?<br>Learn it bitch, our countries are NOT the same.<br>You think you got us tamed?<br>We make the world see you hang heads in shame.<br>In front of 6 billion people<br>we'll take out your Olympic flame</p><p>See I'm saying we bring it any time you fucks are ready,<br>But wait a minute...<br>Shit nigger, ain't you dead already?</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486847" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/06/08(Sun)23:57:0<span></span> <span id="norep486847">No.486847</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207540628001.jpg" target="_blank">1207540628001.jpg</a>-(12 KB, 300x298, <span title="thechairmanisnotamused.jpg">thechairmanisnotamused.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207540628001.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="12 KB" md5="" width="126" height="125"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#486799" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;486799</a></span><p>Dead? I'm Mao, you think the grave can hold me?<br>Reaper showed up, I didn't even let him scold me<br>I took my sickle, chopped that nigga apart<br>All he said was WELL, YOU'RE A REAL WORK OF ART</p><p>When the red parade comes to rock your block<br>Leave the gas can inside, nigga you all best carry glocks<br>My crew might enjoy stomping your hippy asses into mud<br>It ain't my kinda communism unless you're dripping with blood</p><p>Mothafucka, don't you talk shit bout the 'lympics<br>That's our shit nigga, don't mess with that bizness<br>You think one of you clowns on fire was a shock<br>I'll use the Olympic torch and burn you all, after I slap you with my cock</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486879" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)00:02:4<span></span> <span id="norep486879">No.486879</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207540968252.jpg" target="_blank">1207540968252.jpg</a>-(18 KB, 328x425, <span title="untitled.jpg">untitled.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207540968252.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="18 KB" md5="" width="97" height="126"></a><blockquote>I am the reaper, and you are both mine<br>Maybe not now, but give it some time<br>I'll wait for the moment and then we'll both see<br>If you can escape eternity<br>The answer is no, but they all fight anyway<br>It don't matter to me, I will have my way<br>Don't fuck with death, bitches. <table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="486927" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)00:09:4<span></span> <span id="norep486927">No.486927</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207541387519.jpg" target="_blank">1207541387519.jpg</a>-(42 KB, 449x600, <span title="449px-WmHTaft.jpg">449px-WmHTaft.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207541387519.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="42 KB" md5="" width="94" height="126"></a><blockquote>Living large, and I do mean large<br>Big Bill Taft is the man in charge<br>Enforcing the law is my paramount priority<br>And bitch slapping trusts with great authority<br>Just ask Bryan, I'm one tough motha-<br>And always lookin' out for my little brown brothas<br>Rockin' the White House got old, of course<br>So I moved on over to run the courts<br>Don't mess with me, I'm one tough playa<br>Or I'll fuck you up like Nicaragua's president Zelaya<br>The baddest motherfucker since Shaft:<br>The name is William Howard Taft</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="487138" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)00:43:4<span></span> <span id="norep487138">No.487138</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207543429770.gif" target="_blank">1207543429770.gif</a>-(4 KB, 90x68, <span title="worker.gif">worker.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207543429770.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="4 KB" md5="" id="gif487138" width="90" height="68"></a><blockquote>You know that guy who once said that war is hell?<br>I bet I this guy was GLA and knew my buddy Jarmen Kell.<br>He gets the rifle and the camo and the ticket to several virgin,<br>But he's got nothing on this worker; I cut beats like a general surgeon.<p>It's after hours and the scuds have stopped fallin<br>But in the the GLA barracks the freedom fighters have just started brawlin<br>Rhymes invadin ya dome as insidious as that infidel bitch black lotus<br>Kell rushing to the stage, knocks me sprawlin but doesn't notice.</p><p>The angry mob is cheering, AKA's are pumping<br>Because Kell has started busting loose<br>he's really got the party jumping<br>From the excitement you'd think that he was pretty damn spruce.</p><p>Now I've been rapping since before the mission,<br>And I can tell you that Kell's beat was a tame composition<br>If you thought my homies were hoppin' on their own volition<br>Now's the time for me to provide some needed exposition.</p><p>Because everyone knows Kell's a very bad man,<br>And that if you badmouth his rhymes<br>He'll put a bullet in your balls and drag your body to his van</p><p>What's coming next for you my friend will not be good times.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="487149" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)00:44:4<span></span> <span id="norep487149">No.487149</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207543487028.jpg" target="_blank">1207543487028.jpg</a>-(3 KB, 90x68, <span title="kell.jpg">kell.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207543487028.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="3 KB" md5="" width="90" height="68"></a><blockquote>When he's done choppin' with his blade,<br>He'll replace your guts with a spare grenade<br>And your life will be the price you paid<br>for raining on Jarmen Kell's parade.<p>I told my General about Kell's twisted ways<br>He's pretty busy micromanaging but hurriedly he says:<br>"You're just as bad, you're a soldier of terror"<br>But I just collect supplies so I'm pretty sure he's in error.</p><p>I will work all day, moving boxes of ammunition<br>And at night they barely feed me, with food that lacks nutrition<br>My feet are bleeding stumps because they won't give me shoes<br>But that's why Kell's got no chance: I've got nothing to lose.</p><p>Though I'm just a peasant, I've got the beats of a giant<br>To my last breath I'll keep the rhythm going, strong and defiant<br>Though I'm sure that damn sand nigger will most likely cap me<br>My life for Allah, he'll never, ever outrap me.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207543493139.jpg" target="_blank">1207543493139.jpg</a>-(19 KB, 351x359, <span title="charlie_manson.jpg">charlie_manson.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207543493139.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="19 KB" md5="" width="123" height="126"></a><blockquote>I'm the first nigga in procalimin' race war<br>Got a gat <table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="487209" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)00:55:4<span></span> <span id="norep487209">No.487209</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207544146755.jpg" target="_blank">1207544146755.jpg</a>-(71 KB, 418x661, <span title="hankpym.jpg">hankpym.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207544146755.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="71 KB" md5="" width="79" height="126"></a><blockquote>Some days are nice, your girl curls up in your lap<br>But when she gets in your grill, a slut needs a slap<br>Don't doubt me, homey, this gangsta knows science<br>Knock your bitch down if she shows you defiance<p>You can beat her all the time, and she ain't leaving<br>Unless she finds a new man, bitch starts cheats<br>Then when you slip and put her in the emergency room<br>Some white knight motherfucker might try to knock you to your doom.<br>Doesn't that fool realize the slut's playing him?<br>Know what she'll do when she's done laying him?<br>She'll leave, and come running right back<br>To Henry Pym, and his trusty pimp smack.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="487286" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)01:05:2<span></span> <span id="norep487286">No.487286</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207544721300.jpg" target="_blank">1207544721300.jpg</a>-(30 KB, 640x360, <span title="untitled.jpg">untitled.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207544721300.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="30 KB" md5="" width="126" height="70"></a><blockquote>I'm a living line of code, any fool can see<br>Hackers on steroids ain't got shit on me<br>Seven proxies is nothing, bitches, don't give me no lip<br>You're nothing to me but another data blip<br>I catch knives bare-handed, bullets get matrixed<br>Just a quick 'cant an all of your shit's fixed<br>Rewrite code, faster than light<br>Reprogram your war in the middle of the fight<br>Just cuz I hack don't mean I take your crap<br>Even IRL I'll make your neck snap<br>Mess with me, son, and shit will get twisted<br>I can reset the world so you never existed<br>I'm normally passive so don't get agressive<br>A blank stare from me is plenty expressive<br>I'm on the job, so don't fuck with me<br>I'm an Integrated Data Sentient Entity</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="487336" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)01:12:4<span></span> <span id="norep487336">No.487336</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207545167235.jpg" target="_blank">1207545167235.jpg</a>-(25 KB, 334x323, <span title="boromir2.jpg">boromir2.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207545167235.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="25 KB" md5="" width="126" height="121"></a><blockquote>Boromir bitches, alive and kicking<br>Oh, whatsa matter, why you tripping?<br>You think a couple arrows drop a pimp like this gent?<br>That'd be like a single bullet stopping Fiddy Cent<br>I just played dead, didn't want the hassle<br>Of chilling out with midget faggots and that Strider asshole<br>What a stupid quest, a suicide run right past Barad-Dur<br>God that Gandalf must be one crazy nigger<br>Those dumb fucks must be halfway to the border<br>Me I know one does not simply walk into Mordor.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="487445" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)01:29:2<span></span> <span id="norep487445">No.487445</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207546163612.jpg" target="_blank">1207546163612.jpg</a>-(16 KB, 246x351, <span title="tesla02.jpg">tesla02.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207546163612.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="16 KB" md5="" width="88" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#487354" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;487354</a></span><br>(Ask, and ye may receive.)<p>What's up, it's Edison's nemesis<br>Tesla, nigga, the master of electrics<br>You know how an apple hit Newton's head<br>And gravity was discovered?<br>I learned all about magnetism<br>From all the pussy I uncovered<br>I'm the AC nigga, straight up dope electric magician<br>Next to me Edison is no skill all ambition<br>Al Gore didn't invent your internets, that credit hog<br>He just found a connected computer and read about them on my blog<br>"Tesla," you're saying, shouldn't you be gone?<br>Ain't you over 100 years old, nigga, what's going on?"<br>Death don't apply to me son, weren't you told?<br>I just invented it because people got boring and old.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489261" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)11:10:4<span></span> <span id="norep489261">No.489261</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207581049151.jpg" target="_blank">1207581049151.jpg</a>-(28 KB, 354x450, <span title="pinocchio1..jpg">pinocchio1..jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207581049151.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="28 KB" md5="" width="99" height="126"></a><blockquote>Off the strings bitch, off the hook too<br>Geppetto never imagined what this boy could do<br>Made of wood nigga, this boy can't feel pain<br>But he can bring it son, again and again<br>If I don't go to school I might turn into a donkey<br>If he messes around, I might cap a honky<br>Straight up nigga, this balla can't tell no lie<br>I got a wood heart and I make haters die<br>Blue fairy bitch telling me to behave<br>But if I turn into a real boy ya'll put me in my grave<br>Like Pac, nigga, I'll be a martyr for rap<br>Wooden pimp nigga, veins full of sap.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489441" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)12:02:5<span></span> <span id="norep489441">No.489441</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207584170077.jpg" target="_blank">1207584170077.jpg</a>-(7 KB, 269x295, <span title="anon.jpg">anon.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207584170077.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="7 KB" md5="" width="114" height="126"></a><blockquote>Hey, who's that, could it be a scientologist?<br>I'm his worse nightmare, call me anonymous<br>Power over nine thousand, I'm the scourge of the internet<br>Because I don't forgive, and I never forget<br>And if you think your shit is serious business<br>I got some bad news, nigga, you ain't gonna like dis<br>Just for the lulz, I might ruin your life<br>Well, your online shit, but it's the same for you types<br>Bawwwwing and bitching, keeping livejournals, jeez<br>Bitch you been laid out by the master of memes.<br>Is that your daughter? Shit, this is better than I planned<br>Me and my homey Pedobear, we'll never get v&amp;.<br>Fox News calls me a cyber terrorist, I'm pervasive like Lolcats<br>Invisible and implacable, I believe in the law of gats<br>Law goes: Whose got the stones, and whose gun is bigger<br>And which brass-balled /b/tard has already pulled the trigger?<br>Blam-blam-blam!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489557" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)12:30:5<span></span> <span id="norep489557">No.489557</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207585850340.jpg" target="_blank">1207585850340.jpg</a>-(19 KB, 364x291, <span title="doug03.jpg">doug03.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207585850340.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="19 KB" md5="" width="126" height="100"></a><blockquote>Damn - why can't a balla get bizzay?<br>Why are bitches getting up in a tizzay?<br>Hey where are all the sluts in this hizzay?<br>Spread em girl, I wanna soak you in jizz-ay<br>Doug is here ladies but he's tired a waiting<br>So started today, Doug start's TAKING!<br>All the hos that blue-balled me<br>I am gonna start calling<br>Invite myself over<br>Give them a good balling<br>Whether they like it not!<br>Whether they beg or scream stop!<br>Doug's showing the ladies<br>How he rocks the cock!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489827" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)13:37:5<span></span> <span id="norep489827">No.489827</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207589878917.jpg" target="_blank">1207589878917.jpg</a>-(30 KB, 284x474, <span title="01.jpg">01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207589878917.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="30 KB" md5="" width="75" height="126"></a><blockquote>Montag:<br>The name is Montag, fireman by trade<br>Burning books, son, that's how I get paid<br>Welcome to the inferno, the fire never goes out<br>But all of a sudden, I'm feeling some doubt<p>It's like, what the hell are we burning books for?<br>Reading Shakespeare's hardly an act of war<br>Literature, science fiction, history and philosophy<br>Supression in a free society is quite a dichotomy</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489831" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)13:39:0<span></span> <span id="norep489831">No.489831</span><blockquote>Beatty:<br>Montag, you fool, must we do this?<br>Can't you keep your mind shut, just get through this?<br>I been where you are, almost didn't come back<br>I'm telling you now, that you don't know jack<br>We're burning books because they don't contain knowledge<br>Just conflicting philosophies, questions with no way to solve 'em<br>Think you need a book son, to tell you what's what?<br>That just ain't so, you know it in your gut.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489835" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)13:39:4<span></span> <span id="norep489835">No.489835</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207589984221.jpg" target="_blank">1207589984221.jpg</a>-(30 KB, 283x471, <span title="02.jpg">02.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207589984221.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="30 KB" md5="" width="75" height="126"></a><blockquote>Montag:<br>They came for the books, but I wasn't a book<br>And they made em illegal, and I ain't a crook<br>But today I brought one home, couldn't help myself<br>And it pried my third eye open, knowledge is wealth<br>Now the house is burning and the mechanical hound's on my trail<br>I'm to be eliminated, no warrant no jury no jail<br>The wife turned me in, the dumb little bitch<br>But I got a flamethrower, finger on the switch.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489839" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)13:40:4<span></span> <span id="norep489839">No.489839</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207590048543.jpg" target="_blank">1207590048543.jpg</a>-(30 KB, 274x473, <span title="03.jpg">03.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207590048543.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="30 KB" md5="" width="72" height="126"></a><blockquote>Beatty:<br>Montag, it's too late son, no reason to fight this<br>You and I were set on this road, I don't doubt it<br>Shall we debate the respective merits<br>Of dead men and poets, sticks versus carrots?<br>You think you're the first man to doubt the path?<br>Shit nigger, don't make me laugh<br>I've burned a thousand men like you, I don't lose sleep<br>And when you're gone we'll bury you de- AAAUGH!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="489845" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)13:41:4<span></span> <span id="norep489845">No.489845</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207590108507.jpg" target="_blank">1207590108507.jpg</a>-(29 KB, 271x466, <span title="04.jpg">04.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207590108507.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="29 KB" md5="" width="73" height="126"></a><blockquote>Montag:<br>Oh snap, did my flames interrupt your soliloquoy?<br>Burned you alive, dag, wasn't that silly of me?<br>If you were better read then you wouldn't need a coroner<br>Dumbass, you never trap a rat in a corner<br>Send on the hounds, you cunts, there's no stopping me<br>A open mind and a ready fist, shit no topping me<br>One of your own, but out of the fold<br>Head full of knowledge, I remember Rome<br>The empire declined, the empire fell<br>All things must pass, shit can't you tell?<br>You can't burn truth, can't put it in a cell<p>Censor a whisper and you make it a yell.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>
