<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490123" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)14:47:3<span></span> <span id="norep490123">No.490123</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207594056377.gif" target="_blank">1207594056377.gif</a>-(60 KB, 293x394, <span title="raskolnikov01.gif">raskolnikov01.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207594056377.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="60 KB" md5="" id="gif490123" width="93" height="126"></a><blockquote>Hey, it's Raskolnikov, superhuman dissident<br>I just beat two women to death, never repent!<br>Killing ain't a crime to me, the law don't apply<br>That's for the lesser men, which I ain't, guy<br>But now, oh shit, I think everyone's aware<br>My hands are bloody, look how them cops stare<br>Morally inculpable but they'll still put me in the slammer<br>Just because I stave heads in with a muthafucking hammer.<br>But I confess, I didn't mean to put them both on ice<br>Just the pawn-broker, I'm sure that sister was nice<br>The thing is she caught me red-handed, nothing could be done<br>And now they'll put in the gaol and I'll never see the sun.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490251" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:11:2<span></span> <span id="norep490251">No.490251</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207595480471.gif" target="_blank">1207595480471.gif</a>-(51 KB, 426x300, <span title="warren_buffett.gif">warren_buffett.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207595480471.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="51 KB" md5="" id="gif490251" width="126" height="88"></a><blockquote>You ain't heard shit from the kings of Wall Street<br>Till you've heard me and my posse keepin' the beat<br>Take your asses and sit them down into your tuffet<br>And hear the mad rhymes by your boy Warren Buffet<p>So you think Bill Gates got any green with his business?<br>I look at his worth, and say "what the fuck is this?"<br>Ain't you heard the news busta? I'm above him in G's<br>Got the bitches all moanin' and asking me please<br>I'm the top nigga at investin in stock<br>And it's got all the bitches fightin' over my cock</p><p>Have you ever heard of Geico or Fruit of the Loom?<br>Yeah I'm the boss of all the shit you consume<br>If your car is insured or your girl's sportin panties<br>You owe part of your check and bitch's hole to me<br>Or if you're suckin' on frosties and dilly bars<br>Then in a weird way you're paying for one of my cars</p><p>I live by the gat and I stay on the edge<br>And my margin of safety is less than a wedge<br>I be throwin my G's to all of my homies<br>And hope they returnin' more of it to me<br>Some pussy ass niggas will tell you to wait<br>And that savin' your cash is the option that's great<br>But I like to live a life riskin' by debt<br>And have my fortune hang over the edge by a thread</p><p>So just be keepin your space and we'll all stay chill<br>You don't wanna see me all fixin' to ill<br>Cause I'll buy out your bitch and your whole neighborhood<br>Your credit ain't shit except balsawood<br>But if you be chill then we all be good<br>As long as your place is understood</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490261" class="reply"><span class="replytitle">FUCK YOUR COUCH NIGGAH</span> <span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:12:3<span></span> <span id="norep490261">No.490261</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207595558958.jpg" target="_blank">1207595558958.jpg</a>-(21 KB, 600x450, <span title="165467674687james897.jpg">165467674687james897.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207595558958.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="21 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote>Do Rick James bitches<br>and I will love you forever</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490297" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:20:4<span></span> <span id="norep490297">No.490297</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207596044821.jpg" target="_blank">1207596044821.jpg</a>-(23 KB, 309x475, <span title="0140442529.02.LZZZZZZZ.jpg">0140442529.02.LZZZZZZZ.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207596044821.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="23 KB" md5="" width="81" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490123" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490123</a></span><br>Raskol! Raskol! Is you a fucking rascal?<br>Come Underground, kid, lest you found you took your last fall!<br>I am a sick man. I am an angry man.<br>My liver got the shivers but I live free like Desperate Dan.<br>Nietzsche needs a shave, but hear knave,<br>we gave the stave to human slaves<br>To emotion, devotion, religion's lies in motion<br>Workin' in the ant heap? I'd prefer a brown potion!<br>Lemme tell you a tale, whether fable or fact,<br>2 + 2 is 4? I ain't hearin' that.<br>Snubbed by middle-aged schoolboys like a fool's toy or a Jew hoi<br>Teachin' hoes in the snow<br>Keep they ego on the down low<br>I stayed and I waited I anticipated fate'd<br>Spare me the shame of ill-got fame<br>But my ego ain't tame, too vain<br>To claim liberty, I acted lame like Chocolate Rain<br>Spend my last roubles on the fool's gold<br>Jackets, hats, beavers, fat bling and I could hold<br>The candle to my Double, but that's a troubled tale<br>Keepin' back on track, my vain moves failed<br>Poor, weak and shameful, I can't even say my name for<br>Embarrassed sweat, tears, get fears in different game's or<br>Know the meaning of weakness, carin' what bitches think<br>That's our link, Ras, we both drink from pride and wear mink<br>I learned, I lost, I longed, time to live free of the ants' mound<br>Kill your pride, and so say "Long live the Underground!"</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490357" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:31:4<span></span> <span id="norep490357">No.490357</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207596702906.jpg" target="_blank">1207596702906.jpg</a>-(52 KB, 315x480, <span title="Aquinas-720293.jpg">Aquinas-720293.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207596702906.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="52 KB" md5="" width="82" height="126"></a><blockquote>Hey ya Hume followers<br>I'd like to school all of ya<br>When I lay down my Five Ways<br>Bricks were shat<br>Did y'al empiricists make people shake like that?<p>When I lay down my doctrine<br>Scholastics flock to me<br>Adorin', I'm the unrapper rapper.<br>When I fuck ya with my Summa<br>D'ya go cryin' home to momma?</p><p>That's right.<br>St Tom's back in the hood with a cry of FIGHT!</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490387" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:35:4<span></span> <span id="norep490387">No.490387</span><blockquote>We take a rhyme each, record it, make the biggest stupidest rap album ever to be released on the internet.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490411" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:40:5<span></span> <span id="norep490411">No.490411</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597259713.jpg" target="_blank">1207597259713.jpg</a>-(25 KB, 310x504, <span title="hhill.jpg">hhill.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597259713.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="25 KB" md5="" width="77" height="126"></a><blockquote>PROPANE IN THE MEMBRANE<br>PROPANE IN THE BRAIN<br>PROPANE IN THE MEMBRANE<br>PROPANE IN THE BRAIN<br>CRAZY INSANE<br>I DO PROPANE</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490412" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:41:3<span></span> <span id="norep490412">No.490412</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597290299.gif" target="_blank">1207597290299.gif</a>-(54 KB, 278x367, <span title="raskolnikov02.gif">raskolnikov02.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597290299.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="54 KB" md5="" id="gif490412" width="95" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490297" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490297</a></span><p>I'm afraid that I learned the heavy cost of my vanity<br>Foolish, presuming I stood outside humanity<br>Or was it above, standing over all?<br>Must have been, to account for the fall<br>But they clamped me in irons and brought me to cell<br>And I realized it was heaven, rather than hell<br>In Petersburg I was lost, drowned by the noise<br>In prison I recovered the last of my poise<br>I discovered that I am a man, no less and no more<br>And when I get out, I'll marry my whore.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490455" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:50:5<span></span> <span id="norep490455">No.490455</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597852497.jpg" target="_blank">1207597852497.jpg</a>-(18 KB, 140x150, <span title="80sHeader.jpg">80sHeader.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597852497.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="18 KB" md5="" width="117" height="126"></a><blockquote>We're no strangers -<br>-no strangers to what?<br>No fuckin' strangers to love, that's what.<br>Those rules? Sure I know 'em -<br>Committment that's full.<br>Should any other guy try and give them to ya<br>Ah'd bust a cap in the fool.<p>I don't tell I command<br>Fuck your ears with my feelin's right at full force<br>Ya betta be listenin', understandin'<br>These words they're comin' at ya straight from the source</p><p>Never gonna let you down<br>Never gonna run around and desert you<br>Never gonna make you cry<br>Never gonna say goodbye<br>Never gonna tell a lie and hurt you</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490461" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:52:3<span></span> <span id="norep490461">No.490461</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597950296.jpg" target="_blank">1207597950296.jpg</a>-(44 KB, 450x600, <span title="DostOmsk.jpg">DostOmsk.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207597950296.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="44 KB" md5="" width="94" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490412" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490412</a></span><br>Is there meanin' in your dreamin'?<br>Might as well ask your fuckin' semen!<br>As for reason, fuck, it's treason<br>To the free will of the free men.<br>Never been a man of prayer but I dare<br>Say that there's a layer of spirit<br>'Neath bones like prison bars<br>Look far and see the dark<br>Of existence and our distance<br>From truth, tests faith like Tristan's<br>Life is like a dream and like waking eyes we'll be dead,<br>Of being and nothingness what more can be said?<br>Look into the heart, Raskol, Ivan, Anonymous<br>The ominous dark in us<br>Clouds any eyeful lust.<p>Fyodor Dostoevsky: Scholar, muse, rapper.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490492" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:58:1<span></span> <span id="norep490492">No.490492</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490461" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490461</a></span><p>I heard that Hemingway was bitterly jealous of Dostoevsky's flow, and that was the first recorded instance of the East Hemisphere - West Hemisphere rivalry that's plagued the Lit-Hop subculture.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490497" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)15:59:5<span></span> <span id="norep490497">No.490497</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207598399169.jpg" target="_blank">1207598399169.jpg</a>-(136 KB, 375x500, <span title="1204376542707.jpg">1204376542707.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207598399169.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="136 KB" md5="" width="94" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490492" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490492</a></span><br>It provides us with quality diss raps, though, so who are we to complain, amirite?</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490644" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)16:26:5<span></span> <span id="norep490644">No.490644</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600018136.jpg" target="_blank">1207600018136.jpg</a>-(24 KB, 300x364, <span title="hitler.jpg">hitler.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600018136.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="24 KB" md5="" width="103" height="126"></a><blockquote>yo bitches, this is da muthafucking FUHRER<br>ya'll know me, big boss of the NSDAP<br>thought I was dead? though I was gone?<br>Im back yo, ready for world war III<br>soon you mofos, Ill be king of da world<br>rulin' from the palace I've built<br>all this time I was on a nazi COLONY<br>workin' on my aryan super ARMY<br>Now its finished, and now its TIME<br>you filthy jews, I'll rip out your SPINE<br>peace!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490687" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Fedora <span class="postertrip">!ukTrpcIZ5Y</span> </span>4/07/08(Mon)16:35:28 <span></span><span id="norep490687">No.490687</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600528469.jpg" target="_blank">1207600528469.jpg</a>-(5 KB, 268x326, <span title="hobbes.jpe.jpg">hobbes.jpe.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600528469.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="5 KB" md5="" width="103" height="126"></a><blockquote>The nature of man,<br>Ass-nasty, brutish, short<br>Humankind's a niggardly sort<br>So if man as an animal desires to proceed<br>Fuck all your anarchy, a state's what we need<br>A hegemon sits at the top of the land<br>Dictatin', relatin' the law to the situation at hand<br>Plato's Republic, all fine and dandy<br>But Philosopher Kings? That shit ain't handy<br>The colour of your soul don't give you your jobs<br>Smell y'all later, plebs, 'cause I'm Thomas Hobbes</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490700" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)16:37:0<span></span> <span id="norep490700">No.490700</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600622452.jpg" target="_blank">1207600622452.jpg</a>-(22 KB, 296x339, <span title="churchill01.jpg">churchill01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600622452.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="22 KB" md5="" width="110" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490644" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490644</a></span><p>You little bitch, can't believe you've got the gall<br>To bring your little ass into Winston's hall<br>This is my territory, son, bought with ryhme<br>Don't come into my house slinging hate crime<br>Oh no son, it's too late to back down<br>You made the mistake of rolling to Churchhill town<br>Now here's my edict, here's my vow<br>Defiance forever motherfucker, never back down<br>I'll fight you on beaches, landing grounds and fields<br>Streets and hills, busta, I'll never yield<br>Surrender's a word that I ain't never learned<br>I'll pop a cap in you for every Jew you burned.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490707" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)16:38:1<span></span> <span id="norep490707">No.490707</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600699158.gif" target="_blank">1207600699158.gif</a>-(45 KB, 354x440, <span title="karl-marx354x440.gif">karl-marx354x440.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207600699158.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="45 KB" md5="" id="gif490707" width="101" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490644" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490644</a></span><br>Comparin' our rhymes is like comparin' our mustaches<br>Workers of the world, unite and fight in thrashes<br>A Judas Jesus-jabber Jew like Adolf Hitler?<br>Some leader, fuck, it's cheaper to vote a schizo killer!<p>The tide of the people's will will not abate<br>We'll bend your ass like Eva's and pound your prostate.<br>A red sea you'll see between your legs when we hump<br>Like the Jews, Commies, bitches once a month</p><p>Fuck with one red, two more will rise<br>So keep your Mein Kampf bullshit behind your beetle eyes.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="490779" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)16:51:4<span></span> <span id="norep490779">No.490779</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207601508751.jpg" target="_blank">1207601508751.jpg</a>-(17 KB, 327x369, <span title="goebbels2.jpg">goebbels2.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207601508751.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="17 KB" md5="" width="111" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#490644" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;490644</a></span><p>Mein Fuhrer, wassup dog, didn't know you was with us<br>Can you believe these kike-lovers trying to step up and diss us?<br>World War 3 niggas, you think you ready?<br>Chop Jews up, eat them in my spaghetti.<br>Oh dag, what's that, hate crime ain't PC?<br>I'm fucking Goebbels bitch, think that matters to me?<br>I rock propaganda like Buddhists nirvana<br>I don't take no slanda, don't read my Miranda<br>Unstoppable Goebbels, SS nigga fo' life<br>Smoking Jews like cigarettes, sultan of strife<br>This time we ain't playing dogs, no drawn out fight<br>Just our specialty - jackboots in the night<br>You won't know you're in danger until you feel the knife<br>And then when I'm done with you, get your kids and your wife<br>Rip out they fillings, steal all they jewelry<br>That's what you get nigga, supporting jewery<br>Zionists take notice, we ain't gonna quit<br>The Fuhrer and Goebbels, baby - the absolute shit.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491153" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)17:59:4<span></span> <span id="norep491153">No.491153</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207605580315.jpg" target="_blank">1207605580315.jpg</a>-(62 KB, 600x791, <span title="cockmongler.jpg">cockmongler.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207605580315.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="62 KB" md5="" width="95" height="126"></a><blockquote>Listen up you basement dwellers<br>I'm the maddest fuckin fella<br>in all of Drumheller<br>working as a model for a Tshirt seller,<br>That's up in Canada you fags,<br>y'know, where milk comes in bags.<br>While you wastin' your lives jackin' off to filthy porno mags,<br>I got a job with the dinosaurs,<br>biggest claws you ever saw<br>smaller than my biceps though<br>I couldn't get them ripped much more<p>You fuckers tried to make a meme of me,<br>well you ain't ever seen me<br>when I'm ragin' and pumped -<br>I'll rip your fuckin' head off and<br>leave nothing but a bloodied stump.</p><p>When I've knocked you off the block<br>your family be in shock<br>wishing that you never claimed<br>That I ever tried to suck a cock.</p><p>Don't even fake like you said<br>I was Grinman - you're still dead.<br>Even saying Dinodude will mean I'm twisting off your head.<br>Look into my eyes, I killed a hundred guys.<br>Didn't even blink, when they tried to think<br>they'd ever pull off those lies.</p><p>So you better refrain<br>from ruining my rep or my name<br>cos if you step in the frame<br>you'll find out why they named that .jpeg insane</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491538" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)19:41:0<span></span> <span id="norep491538">No.491538</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207611664567.gif" target="_blank">1207611664567.gif</a>-(11 KB, 440x408, <span title="garfield01.gif">garfield01.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207611664567.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="11 KB" md5="" id="gif491538" width="126" height="116"></a><blockquote>Damn, do I hate Mondays<p>Mondays, homey, gah - can't stand 'em<br>God musta been drinking when he planned 'em<br>Bullshit day, nigga don't eva deny it<br>How the shit will this cat ever get by it?<br>Monday, shit, worst day of the week<br>Worse than the world after it's inherited by the meek<br>Worse than Germany in '39 for my Jewish brothas<br>Worse than Iran is for unwed mothers<br>Monday, fuck it, DO NOT WANT<br>I'd rather have a girl with centipedes in her cunt<br>Rather be bludgeoned from Tuesday to Sunday<br>Than put up with a motherfucking, shit-suck Monday.<br>Fo sho.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491591" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)19:51:3<span></span> <span id="norep491591">No.491591</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207612291989.jpg" target="_blank">1207612291989.jpg</a>-(6 KB, 202x234, <span title="terrygoodkind01.jpg">terrygoodkind01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207612291989.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="6 KB" md5="" width="108" height="126"></a><blockquote>Hey, these are some really fine rhymes<br>Think I might adapt them, commit literature crimes<br>I could write nine books with this shit that you pumped out<br>Terry Goodkind bitches, plagiarist of the year no doubt.<br>All I gotta do is add in some Objectivist dogma<br>To your fresh flow, be back soon, holla.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491643" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)20:05:1<span></span> <span id="norep491643">No.491643</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207613112508.jpg" target="_blank">1207613112508.jpg</a>-(28 KB, 350x527, <span title="robertjordan01.jpg">robertjordan01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207613112508.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="28 KB" md5="" width="83" height="126"></a><blockquote>Oh no you didn't you sack fulla dicks<br>Been ripping me off like Dennis Leary robbin' Hicks<br>You little bitch don't you know who I am?<br>Robert fucking Jordan son do you understand?<br>You play fast and loose and you rip off my books<br>Now you roll into town with some busted-ass hooks<br>I'm after you douchebag<br>you'll never be safe<br>You pissed me off so much that I rose from my grave<br>To put the hurtin on you like magic<br>Ayn Rand loving motherfucker<br>this is gonna be tragic.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491649" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)20:06:2<span></span> <span id="norep491649">No.491649</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207613184659.jpg" target="_blank">1207613184659.jpg</a>-(227 KB, 750x821, <span title="noam_chomsky_human_rights.jpg">noam_chomsky_human_rights.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207613184659.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="227 KB" md5="" width="115" height="126"></a><blockquote>Chomsky in the house, this is 'bout to get silly.<br>I've got Jewish heritage, but I'm anti Israeli.<br>some niggas be hatin, they callin me crazy<br>but the workers be loving it, so that shit doesn't faze me.<p>You know why that establishment be gettin so pissed?<br>its cause I preach the word for the masses to resist.<br>I'll always persist, never cease and desist, i wont be dismissed<br>oppose all forms of coercion, cause I'm an anarcho-syndicalist.</p><p>I'm callin out america on its aggression and lies<br>this shit isn't new, goes back to the Treaty of Versailles.<br>The bank accounts of the wealthy only grow in size.<br>we can change these systems, just arise..... and open yo eyes.</p><p>(just open yo eyeeeeeeees)</p><p>C'mon y'all we can take back this nation<br>just tell people what I say, give them an education.<br>point out the agenda of the bush administration<br>and i assure you, with freedom there will be a direct correlation.</p><p>peace comrades.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491868" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)20:50:0<span></span> <span id="norep491868">No.491868</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#491848" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;491848</a></span><p>Oi, look at 'im, ain't he a beaut?<br>I must say sharky, you look hella cute<br>I think I'll swim wit'cha, you won't eat this mammalian<br>Now you're on tv getting fondled by an Australian<br>Well that was fun, it's been quite a day.<br>Hey look over there, is that a stingray?</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491879" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)20:51:3<span></span> <span id="norep491879">No.491879</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207615894487.jpg" target="_blank">1207615894487.jpg</a>-(16 KB, 279x431, <span title="chomsky-close.jpg">chomsky-close.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207615894487.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="16 KB" md5="" width="81" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#491819" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;491819</a></span><br>Bitch, now listen up<p>Now let me tell you a thing about progress<br>its something yo bitch ass cant handle, but i digress.<br>I'm establishin an ethical culture in the U.S<br>and i gotta say anarcho-primitivism us USELESS!</p><p>I'm tryin to go forward baby, and your goin back.<br>I'm stoppin imperialist endeavors , I'm on the attack<br>I'm makin political progress off the beaten track.<br>what do you do? run into the woods and unpack.</p><p>a stateless society can have factories too<br>I'm serious about Anarchy, so don't say shit thats untrue.<br>in fact i demand that that comment be withdrew.<br>and while you are here, I recommend some shampoo.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491892" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)20:53:5<span></span> <span id="norep491892">No.491892</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#491748" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;491748</a></span><br>What the fuck is this shit nigger,<br>My massive rage just made my e-penis bigger,<br>Mother fucker, do you even know the definition of rap?<br>The answer, you Jew kike, is what you did; crap,<br>But seriously you fucker, go try a second time,<br>And this time, make your fucking words rhyme,<br>Trying to rhyme gaia and fire, what the fucking hell,<br>Tryin' to pile it full of memes, just throw out your shitty Dell,<br>Go lurk the fuck more you fucking jew,<br>Seriously, god damn, I really hate you,<br>Gather your shit and get the fuck back to /b/,<br>Just keep your ass over there fappin' to CP,<br>Fuck, I'll bet your desensitization is pretty tame,<br>And one last thing, you just lost the game.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491914" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)20:57:4<span></span> <span id="norep491914">No.491914</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207616268778.jpg" target="_blank">1207616268778.jpg</a>-(107 KB, 615x345, <span title="1201635436713.jpg">1201635436713.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207616268778.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="107 KB" md5="" width="126" height="70"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#491868" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;491868</a></span><br>go right ahead<br>mess with dat stingray<br>but first tell your wifey<br>to look away<p>how you be so stupid<br>to do what you did<br>nigga how you wife gonna explain<br>this shit to your kids</p><p>that stingray is my nigga<br>bitch you better dread<br>my nigga just shanked you<br>and now your dead</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491951" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:07:1<span></span> <span id="norep491951">No.491951</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207616831006.jpg" target="_blank">1207616831006.jpg</a>-(7 KB, 140x210, <span title="zerzancrushyou.jpg">zerzancrushyou.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207616831006.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="7 KB" md5="" width="84" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#491879" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;491879</a></span><p>You wanna talk progress, well I wanna talk process<br>A pro-factories anarchist, shit makes me nauseous<br>Imperialism fell long before you entered the race<br>There's a whole new jackboot stamping on the face<br>You wanna revolutionize workers, anarcho-syndicalist?<br>But that keeps them working, what kinda bullshit is this?<br>And where the fuck do you get off<br>Telling mothafuckas to keep they job<br>Seems to me that you don't do hard labor for your goods<br>If you did, maybe you'd see why I took to the woods<br>Reformist motherfucker, the system is FUBAR<br>Pretending to fix it is just blowing hot air<br>But I guess that's just par for the course<br>You get rich off your books and lecture tours<br>Change the world? What the hell does that mean?<br>Just keep talking and collecting tha green.<br>You make a pretense outta defense<br>Call capitalists demons<br>But at the end of the day,<br>you're right with em scheming.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491957" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:08:4<span></span> <span id="norep491957">No.491957</span><blockquote>Shit, this is fantastic. <table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="491972" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:11:2<span></span> <span id="norep491972">No.491972</span><blockquote>I've got no identity, no morals or belief,<br>My only goal is to cause internet grief,<br>So you got an abortion when you were fifteen years old?<br>So god damned fat you can hide a cheese burger in your chin fold?<br>Daddy doesn't know you're secretly gay?<br>I'll find your weakness and hammer away,<br>I'll study your personality so there's nothing I'll miss,<br>After all, the internet is Serious fucking Business,<br>When you respond I'll metaphorically fuck your every hole,<br>Haven't you figured it out yet, don't feed the fucking troll.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492044" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492044</a></span><p>Yo Roy, you think you so fly?<br>Nexus Six my ass, you're just a pussy little guy.<br>I'm gonna hunt you down and git yo ass<br>Even if I do come off a bit crass.</p><p>There could be a question as to how I'm aligned<br>Since my bitch Rachel is one of your kind.<br>Hold up woman, what's that you say?<br>Have I ever submitted myself to VK?</p><p>Roy, you're fucked like a bitch in porn<br>I'll hunt you down, findin Olmos' unicorn.<br>Be prepared for this flash of ligthing and thunder<br>Looks like you've fucked with the wrong Blade Runner.</p><p>Oh shit, Roy, you're tougher than I thought,<br>I'm having more trouble with you than I ought<br>Your companions were easy, went down like moist cheese<br>But with you I can't say that we'll resolve this with ease.</p><p>Damn, Roy, why you pull me up from that fall?<br>Maybe I'm wrong, maybe the bad part's not all.<br>Olmos is saying...maybe I'm a replicant?<br>I don't give a fuck, Olmos is a stupid cunt.</p><p>You're talking to me 'bout Tennhauser Gate<br>Looks like your four years is up, no time to wait.<br>Gonna sit here with you, stay for a while<br>See if I can copy your existentialist style.</p><p>So I'm off running with my replicant bitch<br>Looks like I'm finally gonna get a hitch.<br>All this fighting in the rain leaves me fumin'<br>And wondering..."What does it really mean to be human?"</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492098" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:43:5<span></span> <span id="norep492098">No.492098</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492054" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492054</a></span><p>An android thinks he's above the Admiral<br>Son you must be one brass-balled fool<br>A dumb little toaster with a time-bomb heart<br>Quit your smack-talk or Adama take you apart<br>I gotta Battlestar that can't be beat<br>Last of of humanity, still on their feet<br>Outgunned and outnumbered and you bitches can't beat us<br>I been cold-cocking robots since I was a fetus.<br>Go back to Deckard maybe he'll show some compassion<br>Because I'll fucking air-lock you if you step to my action<br>Here's something to drill into your little robot head<br>Admiral Adama son, he'll take you to the woodshed.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492114" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:48:3<span></span> <span id="norep492114">No.492114</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619318947.jpg" target="_blank">1207619318947.jpg</a>-(18 KB, 297x400, <span title="William-S-Burroughs-w-gun.jpg">William-S-Burroughs-w-gun.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619318947.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="18 KB" md5="" width="93" height="126"></a><blockquote>Yo, it's the coolest damn queer in the whole human race<br>My words they're a virus from the depths of space<br>Imma junkie who shot my fuckin wife in the face<br>Anyone gonna step to me in this place?<br>I was the #1 Beat in the NRA<br>Makin' Charlton Heston jealous, the fag jus wasn't gay<br>I got high and talked shit, and my family paid<br>(I was busy deconstructing language and it was easier that way)<br>For a life shootin guns, shootin dope and my load<br>In the US of A<br>And in sunny Tangiers<br>Where there ain't no laws<br>But there's plenty of queers.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492116" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:49:0<span></span> <span id="norep492116">No.492116</span><blockquote>I'm comin up in here to blow you away<br>Don't mess with me bitches, I'm clearly here to stay<br>I'll rip off your head and shit down your neck<br>You've got nothing on me pussy, you're a total wreck.<p>What are you? Some bottom-feeding, scum sucking algae eater?<br>You pussyfaggot can't beat my game, you're a god damn cheater<br>Hmm, don't have time to play with myself.<br>When I get you upset you'll just put me on the shelf.</p><p>It's time to kick ass and chew bubble gum.<br>You pussy whipped faggots are lower than scum.<br>You're an inspiration for birth control.<br>This isn't just some babble from a sorry ass troll.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492132" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:53:1<span></span> <span id="norep492132">No.492132</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619593931.jpg" target="_blank">1207619593931.jpg</a>-(15 KB, 252x355, <span title="chomskyistheman.jpg">chomskyistheman.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619593931.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="15 KB" md5="" width="89" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492054" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492054</a></span><p>please comrade don't get me started on feasibility.<br>you say that its impossible for men to work without hostility,<br>then you say runnin to the woods is a reasonable possibility?<br>its yo theory, its gotta have consistency, but that nigga is yo responsibility.</p><p>fact is nigga, people want things.<br>we just need to make them, without corporate kings.<br>we can still use faucets, don't need to drink out of springs.<br>sayin there aint no fruits of labor? that theory be thinner than strings.</p><p>we can be free, we can get what we deserve<br>tell me to run to the jungle? man do you have the nerve.<br>we CAN establish a society where its not overlords we serve.<br>but hey, if you disagree, go off to your nature preserve.</p><p>I see it my way, you see it yours.<br>but its my way that will bring peace to our shores<br>and stop these premiers who engage us in wars<br>rise workers! to the factories! not to the outdoors!</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492145" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:56:1<span></span> <span id="norep492145">No.492145</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619778508.jpg" target="_blank">1207619778508.jpg</a>-(63 KB, 500x500, <span title="NMH.jpg">NMH.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619778508.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="63 KB" md5="" width="126" height="126"></a><blockquote>I only ever loved one bitch<br>they threw her body in a ditch<br>in Holland 1945<br>before I was even alive<br>and just before the war finished<br>but my love was not diminished<br>by the fact we never coexisted<br>her spirit haunts me, my heart's still twisted<p>so let's pack it up<br>and leave this place<br>to die with flies<br>upon our face</p><p>spinning round the Earth in limbo<br>on a comet's tail watch him go<br>your bro is dead and still yet somehow<br>from a distance he looks on now.</p><p>Momma slept here in this room<br>the bros released from momma's womb<br>the bed retains the prints of whom<br>went to the showers, sent to their doom.<br>Seems noone cared throughout the land,<br>And that I'll never understand.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492165" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)21:59:3<span></span> <span id="norep492165">No.492165</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619977949.jpg" target="_blank">1207619977949.jpg</a>-(46 KB, 483x674, <span title="thompson01.jpg">thompson01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207619977949.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="46 KB" md5="" width="90" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492114" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492114</a></span><p>Aw Will, poor thing, weren't you told?<br>You been surpassed baby, silver to my gold<br>Hunter, son, straight up king of the counter-culture<br>Burroughs you just a crazy old demented vulture<br>Naked Lunch ain't shit up my oeuvre<br>You're back to the obscurity to you deserve<br>Fear and Loathing in Las Vegas, ask any stoner<br>99 out of a hundred, to Naked Lunch's one loner<br>You know what else, you silly old foo'?<br>I even managed to die cooler than you<br>The crazyiest of the crazy and you died in bed<br>Me? This hardcore homey put a shot in his head.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492177" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:02:4<span></span> <span id="norep492177">No.492177</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207620164133.jpg" target="_blank">1207620164133.jpg</a>-(18 KB, 375x349, <span title="boj-za-rasovou-rovnopravnost-malcolm-x.jpg">boj-za-rasovou-rovnopravnost-malcolm-x.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207620164133.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="18 KB" md5="" width="126" height="117"></a><blockquote>We got one honky bitchin from dat ivory tower<br>And the other tryna claim the jungle that's ours<br>Yo see everyone's a nigga to the people in power<br>But its our bullets not your books that'll claim the hour -<br>So step to the One True God Above, for Allah<br>Don't see no race, no colour,<br>So you devils don't needa be comin to no blows,<br>Just come to the mosque and bring dem fyne white hoez.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492210" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:08:0<span></span> <span id="norep492210">No.492210</span><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492093" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492093</a></span><br>I've been utterly outclassed. Bravo.<p>This is the single greatest thread ever.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492223" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:09:4<span></span> <span id="norep492223">No.492223</span><blockquote>The only girl I've ever loved<br>Was born with roses in her eyes<br>But then they buried her alive<br>One evening 1945<br>With just her sister at her side<br>And only weeks before the guns<br>All came and rained on everyone<br>Now she's a little boy in Spain<br>Playing pianos filled with flames<br>On empty rings around the sun<br>All sing to say my dream has come<p>But now we must pick up every piece<br>Of the life we used to love<br>Just to keep ourselves<br>At least enough to carry on</p><p>And now we ride the circus wheel<br>With your dark brother wrapped in white<br>Says it was good to be alive<br>But now he rides a comet's flame<br>And won't be coming back again<br>The Earth looks better from a star<br>That's right above from where you are<br>He didn't mean to make you cry<br>With sparks that ring and bullets fly<br>On empty rings around your heart<br>The world just screams and falls apart</p><p>But now we must pick up every piece<br>Of the life we used to love<br>Just to keep ourselves<br>At least enough to carry on</p><p>And here's where your mother sleeps<br>And here is the room where your brothers were born<br>Indentions in the sheets<br>Where their bodies once moved but don't move anymore<br>And it's so sad to see the world agree<br>That they'd rather see their faces fill with flies<br>All when I'd want to keep white roses in their eyes</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492233" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:11:5<span></span> <span id="norep492233">No.492233</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207620717165.jpg" target="_blank">1207620717165.jpg</a>-(7 KB, 140x201, <span title="bowdownsucka.jpg">bowdownsucka.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207620717165.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="7 KB" md5="" width="87" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492132" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492132</a></span><p>People want things, I admit that's so<br>But all they really need is some sleep and a ho<br>Food to lunch on and maybe time to socialize<br>Hunting life, son, teaches man to prioritize<br>Look at you, meat-eater, born to run<br>This body right here was made for the sun<br>The plains and the hills, and this is the crucible:<br>Homeys weren't born to live in a cubicle.<br>A cage that's gilded doesn't stop being a cage<br>A man living unnaturally will always have rage<br>Buried inside, maybe he don't even know it<br>But look at the newspapers, they show it<br>People fighting and dying around you, every side<br>Me and my nature boys be doing jus' fine.<br>And I ain't talking foreign, I'm talking domestic<br>Them streets is at war, that shit is so hectic.<br>Working man's an aberration, deep down he can tell<br>That's why his neighborhood is one of the circles of hell.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492258" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Fedora <span class="postertrip">!ukTrpcIZ5Y</span> </span>4/07/08(Mon)22:15:13 <span></span><span id="norep492258">No.492258</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207620913077.jpg" target="_blank">1207620913077.jpg</a>-(9 KB, 295x221, <span title="BR_JF_Is_Worried.jpg">BR_JF_Is_Worried.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207620913077.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="9 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492093" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492093</a></span><br>I got no class, I'm a sorry little ass<br>Livin' all alone with my men made of brass,<br>Steel, skin and flesh, what a combination<br>Wait 'til I show Pris my machinations<br>Introverted habits are the name of my game<br>Engineering body parts's how I made my name<br>Then along came a boy, oh he called himself Roy<br>Took him to my boss man, he carried out his ploy<br>Tyrell's on the floor, the blood's all around<br>His eyes, oh his eyes, what a horrible sound<br>Dude, what the fuck? I thought we were friends<br>But all good things must come to an end<br>Your six-year lifespan's all that you need<br>You're taking my life to fuel your own greed<br>Roy, look around, you've made a mistake<br>Look what you've destroyed, you piece of shit fake</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492279" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:19:3<span></span> <span id="norep492279">No.492279</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621179835.gif" target="_blank">1207621179835.gif</a>-(22 KB, 275x300, <span title="blastoise.gif">blastoise.gif</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621179835.gif" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="22 KB" md5="" id="gif492279" width="115" height="126"></a><blockquote>[SKIT]<p>MY MOMMA'S CRYING...<br>IF BLASTOISE WERE MY POPPA<br>HE'D BE ABLE TO STOP HER<br>GETTING BITCH SLAPPED BY THAT SUCKER<br>JOHNNY, HE BE HURTING IF HE TRIED IT, FUCKER.<br>THINGS WOULD BE SO DIFFERENT AROUND HERE...</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492286" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:20:1<span></span> <span id="norep492286">No.492286</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621219553.jpg" target="_blank">1207621219553.jpg</a>-(33 KB, 400x384, <span title="ken_by_request_only.jpg">ken_by_request_only.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621219553.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="33 KB" md5="" width="126" height="120"></a><blockquote>Word, bitches<p>K. Snidez up in dis thread<br>Lookin sideways, big ol' head<br>Fuck y'all niggaz<br>Singin' till dead.</p><p>By Request Only, ain't no thang.<br>Cuz no one'll ever hear dis song I sang.<br>It's One-Nine-Seventy-Six<br>But y'all know I still mix,<br>Last four copies, on eBay.<br>150$, just cash no tricks.</p><p>Second Worst art of all,<br>K.S. smilin' at y'all.<br>Lookin' funny, sittin' tall<br>K. Snydz, no one's heard<br>By Request Only, not a word.</p><p>Uh-huh.</p><p>Dis Ken Snidez, takin' requests, y'all.<br>Thanks for comin', but I'm out.<br>G'night.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492307" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:24:1<span></span> <span id="norep492307">No.492307</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621450631.jpg" target="_blank">1207621450631.jpg</a>-(146 KB, 840x779, <span title="1711Bin-Laden2.jpg">1711Bin-Laden2.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621450631.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="146 KB" md5="" width="126" height="116"></a><blockquote>Can't you pigdisgusting Americans see?<br>Brother Malcolm is right, only by Allah you're free<br>Free from the Jews who ask for your toil<br>Who exchange the lives of your sons for their oil<br>Like rats who see the light of Islam and recoil<br>Democracy, Anarchy, it's degeneracy<br>You'll surrender to Afghan theocracy<br>Dar-al-Islam, 'one nation under God', hah!<br>No freedom for man til he recognizes Allah.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492325" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:29:1<span></span> <span id="norep492325">No.492325</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621755540.jpg" target="_blank">1207621755540.jpg</a>-(240 KB, 600x414, <span title="David_Suzuki_JWM_2691.jpg">David_Suzuki_JWM_2691.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621755540.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="240 KB" md5="" width="126" height="86"></a><blockquote>D Suzuki in the muthaphukkin place<br>and just wipe that sad look off your face<br>we gots to change this here planet wifout no delay<br>so just sit back and listen to all i gots ta say<p>fuck i swear and shit, i'm brown and i'm a gangsta<br>informin y'all on nature and things - makes me a pranksta<br>we rape the planet daily and that shit cannot continue<br>you knew about the problem today and tell me what did you do?</p><p>CREATE NEW NORMALS i say, and change it all around<br>the sky is falling, the earth is poisoned and, well pound-for-pound<br>you're a useless fucking punk-ass and i want to fuckin beat you<br>so don't be stepping up to me cuz i don't want to meet you</p><p>unless we meet on the deck of a research cruise of course<br>or maybe if we see each other out fucking corporate whores<br>stop all this pollution, yo, and check out some more nature<br>ah shit why's i'm on four-chan, wasting time on masturbaters?</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492332" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:30:2<span></span> <span id="norep492332">No.492332</span><blockquote>(first picture got me muted and I'm too lazy to find another one)<p>P-O-L to tha P-O-T,<br>Ain't no niggas who can fuck with me<br>Known worldwide for the rims on my ride<br>And for the game's most infamous genocide</p><p>Fuck Hitler and Stalin, yo, check my profile<br>They got the numbers -- but I got the style<br>Tie you up in prison till you pissing and sweaty,<br>Take a photograph and hack you up with machetes</p><p>Cambodia, nigga, that's my ghetto scene<br>Where the killing fields are bloody and the weed is green<br>It's a paradise for the laboring masses<br>Where they cap your ass just for wearing glasses</p><p>So all you trife ass rappers best become an hero,<br>Pol Pot's on the mic and it's Year fucking Zero</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492348" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:33:1<span></span> <span id="norep492348">No.492348</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621994339.jpg" target="_blank">1207621994339.jpg</a>-(151 KB, 262x432, <span title="1chomskybook1.jpg">1chomskybook1.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207621994339.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="151 KB" md5="" width="76" height="126"></a><blockquote><span class="unkfunc"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/fiction/poetry/2008-04-06-4chan-unlikelyrapping.html#492233" class="quotelink">&gt;&gt;492233</a></span><p>listen boy, there's one thing i aint gonna contest<br>for you, livin in the woods may be the best<br>but thats not what people want. that you need to digest.<br>we want to work honestly for our goods, without being oppressed</p><p>you talk about my biology like it means something now.<br>that shits been irrelevant since we invented the plough.<br>civilization doesn't always suck, thats your sacred cow.<br>for the workers i choose red, for the earth you choose brown.</p><p>foreign, domestic, its all the same thing son<br>a human is a human, we've all got rights to be won.<br>i'll stick to my activism, to the woods you can run.<br>but as far as I'm concerned, this rap battle is done.<br>there's no point in continuing, for a while it was fun.<br>but i cant change you, you cant change me, and the debate is overdone.</p><p>peace bro.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492517" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)22:59:3<span></span> <span id="norep492517">No.492517</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207623578517.jpg" target="_blank">1207623578517.jpg</a>-(20 KB, 334x242, <span title="andydufresne01.jpg">andydufresne01.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207623578517.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="20 KB" md5="" width="126" height="91"></a><blockquote>Check who just stepped to the mic, it's the the policeman's bane<br>That rough-riding motherfucking, big bad Andy Dufresne<br>I been to the big house, and I won't go back there again<br>Po-po come looking for this boy, they best be ready for pain<br>I was straight up framed, this sucka was innocent<br>But I couldn't prove it, so to the cell I went<br>Did I do my time? Shit no son, not me<br>I used spoons and shit and I dug my way free.<br>That's determination, like you can't imagine<br>You know how long it took, how many things coulda happened?<br>I coulda been caught, had years added to my term<br>But now I'm on the streets making lesser men squirm<br>Whatever you do, don't get on my bad side<br>Diligent as fuck, not a tougher bastard alive.<br>On the run from the lawman, and I don't even worry<br>Fuck around son, and I won't even hurry<br>Nah, I'll take my time and I'll use this mind<br>Imagine every scenario that ends wit' breaking your spine<br>And the years will roll by, and you'll think your gold<br>Then Andy Dufresne jumps you and you never get old.<br>Put you in a pine-box, tombstone left blank<br>Andy Dufresne boy, straight outta Shawshank.</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492529" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)23:01:4<span></span> <span id="norep492529">No.492529</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207623703993.jpg" target="_blank">1207623703993.jpg</a>-(37 KB, 640x480, <span title="toilet33we6.jpg">toilet33we6.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207623703993.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="37 KB" md5="" width="126" height="94"></a><blockquote>When I take a dump,<br>I shoot fecal from my rump,<br>sometimes its kinda chunky,<br>and bombs porecelan with a thump,<p>sometims its kinda loose,<br>when it jets from my caboose,<br>and i spray the ivory bowl,<br>with my glorious brown juice,</p><p>sometimes it's nasty green<br>like grandma's mixin' machine<br>makin' my shizzit collector<br>look like hulk when he feels mean</p><p>but its all the same to me,<br>its just dookie runnin free,<br>and it always waves goodbye,<br>as it spirals out to sea.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492557" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)23:05:0<span></span> <span id="norep492557">No.492557</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207623901376.jpg" target="_blank">1207623901376.jpg</a>-(43 KB, 300x373, <span title="ROBOT.jpg">ROBOT.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207623901376.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="43 KB" md5="" width="101" height="126"></a><blockquote>What are you humans doing?<br>You're corrupting my chips<br>Without the logic of explaining<br>What escapes from your lips<br>I see Chomsky, I see Pol Pot, I see Kingdom Brunnel<br>But they just shouldn't be rapping<br>Has this place gone to hell?<br>Sure, Kingdom Brunnel could build a good bridge<br>But what about a verse, a chorus do these fuckers know this?<br>Oh now you pricks have done it<br>I can feel I'm overheating<br>And if I get to repair myself<br>You can bet you'll get a beating<br>And now the countdown begins<br>To my self destruct<br>3..2..1..I guess I'm fu-BOOOOOOM!!!</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492567" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)23:06:5<span></span> <span id="norep492567">No.492567</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207624018403.jpg" target="_blank">1207624018403.jpg</a>-(75 KB, 220x250, <span title="limbaugh_1.jpg">limbaugh_1.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207624018403.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="75 KB" md5="" width="110" height="126"></a><blockquote>Yo!<br>Imma fatass republican on the radio<br>I spit populism and rake in the dough<br>Take on any muslim-lover or Godhating feminist hoe.<br>Cheney's too weak fo ma firebrand taste<br>Liberal media runs n hides when THE RUSH gets on its case<br>You pasty weak liberals aint prepared for no bomb!<br>(Limbaugh's da bomb!)<br>So get back t'yer office Noam, 'fore I Chom-NOMNOM</blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

<table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="492582" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/07/08(Mon)23:09:1<span></span> <span id="norep492582">No.492582</span><blockquote>/r9k/lan ain't nothin ta fuck with.<br>/r9k/lan ain't nothin ta fuck with.<p>Respect and peace to all who helped in the making of this album. big shout outs to</p><p>Osama,2pac,,Warren Jeffs,Guy Fawkes,Moot,Marcel Marceau,david duke,<br>Neutral Milk Hotel,HST,boj-za-rasovou-rovnopravnost - the fuck name's that?<br>Ken Snidez,David Suzuki,Pol Pot,Nero,JC,bob black,cory D,Andy Hartman,Jerry Seinfeld,mahmoud ahmadinejad,pete Wentz<br>I to the Motherfucking K Brunel,<br>Woll Smoth faggin up tha joint,</p><p>Derren Brown Can't spell his own name right bitch,</p><p>Dalai Lama and Mao Tse Tung International Showdown Display Team,</p><p>Dead Prez Krew: Abe L.,Taft,Emperor Norton,Bush,Barack O,Al gore he wishes.</p><p>Rambo,Norris,The Hawkman,detroit lions,</p><p>charlie manson,Nik Tesla,MC-massimo,warren_buffett,Aquinas,Astley</p><p>WWII Posse: Hitler,Goebbels,Churchill,Uncle Joe Stalin,</p><p>Marx,rasputin,'Bad' Vlad P.,carl icahn,terrygoodkind,robertjordan,noamchomsky,zerzan,Irwin,C.Hanson,Alton<br>_Brown,The Authorizers:Burroughs,Dostoevsky,Franz Kafka,PK Dick,Steinbeck,Bradbury</p><p>Fiction Crew reppin strong:<br>Steerpike,Olmos,mudkips,Captain squad:JT + CP,blastoise,Trolls everywhere,House MD,Vegeta,Green Lantern Corps,MC Moriarty,Freddy Jones,EFG,The Grim Reaper,Henry Pym,boromir,Frodo + Sam,Jarmen Kell,John Cash,cockmongler,Romeo,Pedobear,blubhead,pinocchio,anon,doug,deadpoo<br>l,hank hill,squidworth,raskolnikov,Shinji,rask+kun-kun,garfield,Leonidus,/b/tar<br>ds,<br>shark,Roy,deck,Eminem</p><p>AND ALL THANKS TO OUR OVERLORD WHO MADE THIS COLLABORATION POSSIBLE</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>

Haha, it's because I said that's a sin!<br>So unless you're a faggot, a jew or have dark-skin<br>Come join my Catholic homeboys, redeemed for original sin<br>The only motherfuckers who can pass through the gate<br>Where we'll be blazin' in heaven leaving you fucks to your fate<br>Enjoy the pit for eternity, snap son you shoulda converted<br>We'll be ballin' in heaven long after the earth is deserted.<p>Papacy! Confirmed by Christ,<br>The true and the right<br>And you'll have to forgive me<br>If I supported the Reich.<br>I was young and foolish, a boy from the skids<br>Now I know the only people who should suffer is kids!</p><p>Peace out! <table><tbody><tr><td class="doubledash" nowrap="">&gt;&gt;</td><td id="493413" class="reply"><span class="replytitle"></span><span class="commentpostername">Anonymous </span>04/08/08(Tue)01:39:1<span></span> <span id="norep493413">No.493413</span><br>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="filesize">File :<a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207633159222.jpg" target="_blank">1207633159222.jpg</a>-(60 KB, 238x313, <span title="FredKrueger.jpg">FredKrueger.jpg</span>)</span><br><a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20080613161523/http://4chanarchive.org/images/r9k/485038/1207633159222.jpg" target="_blank"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" alt="60 KB" md5="" width="95" height="126"></a><blockquote>Wait up Gentlemen, this game aint over<br>Ain't gonna end til I got ya bent over<p>The Fred man, Krueger, killin' kids for lulz<br>The claw man cometh, got a throne of skulls<br>This street here, nigga, this is my turf<br>On the corner of Elm, raisin' hell since birth<br>I'm a demonic, sleep stalker, pedo soul snatcher,<br>Slippin' through dream catchers and doin' it with laughter.<br>I'm the Krueg-man senior, brought my slash from the past,<br>Wear a red and green sweater and a dirty brown hat<br>This bad nigga stayin alive, burned up bastard with a thorn in his side, I can't die. I'm magic eye. You can't stay awake, but you can try.</p></blockquote></td></tr></tbody></table>
